     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     4→import {
     5→  createDecisionRecord,
     6→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     7→  getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage,
     8→  listDecisionConstraintCandidates,
     9→  parseRecordIdString,
    10→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
    11→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
    12→  type GraphEntityRecord,
    13→} from "../../graph/queries";
    14→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    15→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    16→
    17→const resolveDecisionInputSchema = z.object({
    18→  question: z.string().min(1).describe("The decision question"),
    19→  options: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional().describe("Known options being considered"),
    20→  context: z
    21→    .object({
    22→      project: z.string().optional(),
    23→      feature: z.string().optional(),
    24→    })
    25→    .optional()
    26→    .describe("Optional project/feature scope for searching decisions"),
    27→});
    28→
    29→export function createResolveDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    30→  return tool({
    31→    description:
    32→      "Infer an answer to a decision question from graph context. Returns resolved/inferred/unresolved with rationale and sources.",
    33→    inputSchema: resolveDecisionInputSchema,
    34→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    35→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    36→      const queryEmbedding = await createEmbeddingVector(deps.embeddingModel, input.question, deps.embeddingDimension);
    37→      if (!queryEmbedding) {
    38→        throw new Error("failed to create query embedding for resolve_decision");
    39→      }
    40→
    41→      const projectRecord = input.context?.project
    42→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    43→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    44→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    45→            projectInput: input.context.project,
    46→          })
    47→        : undefined;
    48→
    49→      const featureRecord = input.context?.feature
    50→        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
    51→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    52→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    53→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    54→          })
    55→        : undefined;
    56→
    57→      const candidates = await listDecisionConstraintCandidates({
    58→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    59→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    60→        queryEmbedding,
    61→        ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    62→        limit: 10,
    63→      });
    64→
    65→      const decisionCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "decision");
    66→      const topDecision = decisionCandidates[0];
    67→
    68→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.92) {
    69→        const existingDecision = parseRecordIdString(topDecision.id, ["decision"], "decision");
    70→        const resolvedFrom = await getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage({
    71→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    72→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    73→          decisionRecord: existingDecision,
    74→        });
    75→
    76→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    77→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    78→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    79→          targetRecord: existingDecision,
    80→          now: new Date(),
    81→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
    82→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    83→          fromText: input.question,
    84→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    85→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    86→          ...(resolvedFrom ? { resolvedFromRecord: resolvedFrom } : {}),
    87→        });
    88→
    89→        return {
    90→          status: "resolved",
    91→          decision: {
    92→            id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
    93→            name: topDecision.name,
    94→          },
    95→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
    96→          sources: [
    97→            {
    98→              id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
    99→              name: topDecision.name,
   100→              score: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
   101→            },
   102→          ],
   103→        };
   104→      }
   105→
   106→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.78) {
   107→        const now = new Date();
   108→        const sources = candidates.slice(0, 5);
   109→        const basedOn = sources
   110→          .map((source) => {
   111→            try {
   112→              return parseRecordIdString(source.id, [source.kind as "project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question"], source.kind as any);
   113→            } catch {
   114→              return undefined;
   115→            }
   116→          })
   117→          .filter((value): value is GraphEntityRecord => value !== undefined);
   118→
   119→        const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
   120→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   121→          summary: input.question,
   122→          status: "inferred",
   123→          now,
   124→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   125→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   126→          rationale: `Inferred from existing graph context with ${sources.length} related entities.`,
   127→          ...(input.options && input.options.length > 0 ? { optionsConsidered: input.options } : {}),
   128→          ...(basedOn.length > 0 ? { basedOn } : {}),
   129→          inferredBy: "chat_agent",
   130→          decidedByName: "chat_agent",
   131→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
   132→          ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),
   133→        });
   134→
   135→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   136→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   137→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   138→          targetRecord: decisionRecord,
   139→          now,
   140→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
   141→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   142→          fromText: input.question,
   143→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   144→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   145→        });
   146→
   147→        return {
   148→          status: "inferred",
   149→          decision: {
   150→            id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
   151→            name: input.question,
   152→          },
   153→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
   154→          rationale: `Top matching decision: ${topDecision.name}. Related context was strong enough to infer a recommendation.`,
   155→          sources: sources.map((source) => ({
   156→            id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   157→            name: source.name,
   158→            score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   159→          })),
   160→        };
   161→      }
   162→
   163→      return {
   164→        status: "unresolved",
   165→        relatedContext: candidates.slice(0, 6).map((source) => ({
   166→          id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   167→          kind: source.kind,
   168→          name: source.name,
   169→          score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   170→        })),
   171→        suggestProvisional: true,
   172→      };
   173→    },
   174→  });
   175→}
   176→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import {
     5→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     6→  createQuestionRecord,
     7→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     9→} from "../../graph/queries";
    10→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    11→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    12→
    13→export function createCreateQuestionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    14→  return tool({
    15→    description:
    16→      "Create a question entity for open questions that require a choice or pending decision — e.g. \"should we use X or Y?\", \"which approach for Z?\". Do NOT use for informational queries like \"what is blocking X?\" or \"how does Y work?\" — answer those directly or use other tools.",
    17→    inputSchema: z.object({
    18→      text: z.string().min(1).describe("The question text"),
    19→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Question category"),
    20→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important, needs attention soon. medium: normal priority. low: nice-to-have, deferred."),
    21→      context: z
    22→        .object({
    23→          project: z.string().optional(),
    24→          feature: z.string().optional(),
    25→        })
    26→        .optional(),

     8→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,

    15→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";

    17→
    18→export function createCreateWorkItemTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    19→  return tool({
    20→    description:
    21→      "Create a task, feature, or project directly in the knowledge graph. Use when the user explicitly requests creation (\"add a task for X\") or during onboarding entity seeding. For uncertain/brainstormed items, prefer suggest_work_items instead.",
    22→    inputSchema: z.object({
    23→      kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe(
    24→        "task: concrete executable work with action verb. feature: capability/requirement within a project. project: named top-level workstream.",
    25→      ),
    26→      title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise entity title"),
    27→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this entity is needed — seeds the description"),
    28→      category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Category classification"),
    29→      priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important, needs attention soon. medium: normal priority. low: nice-to-have, deferred."),
    30→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Project name to scope the entity under"),
    31→    }),
    32→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    33→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    34→      const now = new Date();
    35→
    36→      const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(
    37→        deps.embeddingModel,
    38→        input.title,
    39→        deps.embeddingDimension,
    40→      );
    41→
    42→      const entityId = randomUUID();
    43→
    44→      if (input.kind === "project") {
    45→        const projectRecord = await createProjectRecord({
    46→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    47→          name: input.title,
    48→          status: "active",
    49→          now,
    50→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    51→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    52→        });
    53→
    54→        if (embedding) {
    55→          await deps.surreal.update(projectRecord).merge({ embedding });
    56→        }
    57→
    58→        void seedDescriptionEntry({
    59→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    60→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    61→          text: input.rationale,
    62→        }).catch(() => undefined);
    63→
    64→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    65→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    66→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    67→          targetRecord: projectRecord,
    68→          now,
    69→          confidence: 0.95,
    70→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    71→          fromText: input.title,
    72→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    73→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    74→        });
    75→
    76→        return {
    77→          entity_id: `project:${projectRecord.id as string}`,
    78→          kind: "project" as const,
    79→          title: input.title,
    80→        };
    81→      }
    82→
    83→      if (input.kind === "task") {
    84→        const taskRecord = new RecordId("task", entityId);
    85→        await deps.surreal.create(taskRecord).content({

    90→          ...(input.priority ? { priority: input.priority } : {}),
    91→          ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),

    94→        });
    95→
    96→        if (input.project) {
    97→          try {
    98→            const projectRecord = await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    99→              surreal: deps.surreal,
   100→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   101→              projectInput: input.project,
   102→            });
   103→            await deps.surreal
   104→              .relate(taskRecord, new RecordId("belongs_to", randomUUID()), projectRecord, {
   105→                added_at: now,
   106→              })
   107→              .output("after");
   108→          } catch {
   109→            // project resolution is best-effort
   110→          }
   111→        }
   112→
   113→        void seedDescriptionEntry({
   114→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   115→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   116→          text: input.rationale,
   117→        }).catch(() => undefined);
   118→
   119→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   120→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   121→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   122→          targetRecord: taskRecord,
   123→          now,
   124→          confidence: 0.95,
   125→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   126→          fromText: input.title,
   127→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   128→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   129→        });
   130→
   131→        return {
   132→          entity_id: `task:${entityId}`,
   133→          kind: "task" as const,
   134→          title: input.title,
   135→        };
   136→      }
   137→
   138→      // feature
   139→      const featureRecord = new RecordId("feature", entityId);
   140→      await deps.surreal.create(featureRecord).content({

   142→        status: "active",
   143→        ...(input.category ? { category: input.category } : {}),
   144→        ...(embedding ? { embedding } : {}),

   149→      if (input.project) {

   155→          });
   156→          await ensureProjectFeatureEdge(deps.surreal, projectRecord, featureRecord, now);
   157→

   165→            },
   166→          }).catch(() => undefined);

   177→

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { ENTITY_CATEGORIES, ENTITY_PRIORITIES, type EntityCategory } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     5→import { resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord, searchEntitiesByEmbedding } from "../../graph/queries";
     6→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     7→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     8→
     9→type SuggestedWorkItem = {
    10→  kind: "task" | "feature" | "project";
    11→  title: string;
    12→  rationale: string;
    13→  category?: EntityCategory;
    14→  project?: string;
    15→  priority?: string;
    16→  possible_duplicate?: {
    17→    id: string;
    18→    name: string;
    19→    similarity: number;
    20→  };
    21→};
    22→
    23→type UpdatedWorkItem = {
    24→  existing_id: string;
    25→  title: string;
    26→  changes: string;
    27→};
    28→
    29→type DiscardedWorkItem = {
    30→  title: string;
    31→  reason: string;
    32→};
    33→
    34→const CLOSED_STATUSES = new Set(["done", "completed", "closed", "resolved", "superseded"]);
    35→
    36→export function createSuggestWorkItemsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    37→  return tool({
    38→    description:
    39→      "Process a batch of proposed work items and return issue-style PM triage buckets: suggestions, updated, discarded.",
    40→    inputSchema: z.object({
    41→      items: z.array(
    42→        z.object({
    43→          kind: z.enum(["task", "feature", "project"]).describe("Work item kind"),
    44→          title: z.string().min(1).describe("Concise work item title"),
    45→          rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why this work item is needed"),
    46→          category: z.enum(ENTITY_CATEGORIES).optional().describe("Optional work item category"),
    47→          project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    48→          priority: z.enum(ENTITY_PRIORITIES).optional().describe("critical: blocking/urgent. high: important. medium: normal. low: nice-to-have."),
    49→        }),
    50→      ).min(1).max(25),
    51→    }),
    52→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    53→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    54→      const suggestions: SuggestedWorkItem[] = [];
    55→      const updated: UpdatedWorkItem[] = [];
    56→      const discarded: DiscardedWorkItem[] = [];
    57→
    58→      for (const item of input.items) {
    59→        const suggestion: SuggestedWorkItem = {
    60→          kind: item.kind,
    61→          title: item.title,
    62→          rationale: item.rationale,
    63→          ...(item.category ? { category: item.category } : {}),
    64→          ...(item.project ? { project: item.project } : {}),
    65→          ...(item.priority ? { priority: item.priority } : {}),
    66→        };
    67→
    68→        const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(deps.embeddingModel, item.title, deps.embeddingDimension);
    69→        if (!embedding) {
    70→          throw new Error("failed to create embedding for suggest_work_items");
    71→        }
    72→
    73→        const projectRecord = item.project
    74→          ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    75→              surreal: deps.surreal,
    76→              workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    77→              projectInput: item.project,
    78→            })
    79→          : undefined;
    80→
    81→        const candidates = await searchEntitiesByEmbedding({
    82→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    83→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    84→          queryEmbedding: embedding,
    85→          kinds: [item.kind],
    86→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
    87→          limit: 5,
    88→        });
    89→
    90→        const top = candidates[0];
    91→        if (!top) {
    92→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    93→          continue;
    94→        }
    95→
    96→        const normalizedTopStatus = top.status ? top.status.trim().toLowerCase() : undefined;
    97→        if (normalizedTopStatus && CLOSED_STATUSES.has(normalizedTopStatus)) {
    98→          suggestions.push(suggestion);
    99→          continue;
   100→        }
   101→
   102→        if (top.score > 0.97) {
   103→          discarded.push({
   104→            title: item.title,
   105→            reason: `Exact duplicate of existing item ${top.kind}:${top.id} (${top.name})`,
   106→          });
   107→          continue;
   108→        }
   109→
   110→        if (top.score >= 0.8) {
   111→          updated.push({
   112→            existing_id: `${top.kind}:${top.id}`,
   113→            title: top.name,
   114→            changes: `Merge context from suggested item '${item.title}'`,
   115→          });
   116→          continue;
   117→        }
   118→
   119→        suggestions.push(suggestion);
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      return {
   123→        suggestions,
   124→        updated,
   125→        discarded,
   126→      };
   127→    },
   128→  });
   129→}
   130→
```

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { SUGGESTION_CATEGORIES } from "../../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { createEmbeddingVector } from "../../graph/embeddings";
     5→import { isEntityInWorkspace, parseRecordIdString } from "../../graph/queries";
     6→import { createSuggestion } from "../../suggestion/queries";
     7→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     8→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     9→
    10→export function createCreateSuggestionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    11→  return tool({
    12→    description:
    13→      "Create a suggestion for the user — a proactive, actionable proposal with rationale. Use when you identify optimizations, risks, opportunities, conflicts, missing elements, or potential pivots that the user should consider. Observations are for agent-to-agent signals; suggestions are agent-to-human proposals.",
    14→    inputSchema: z.object({
    15→      text: z.string().min(1).describe("The suggestion itself — what you propose the user should consider or do"),
    16→      category: z
    17→        .enum(SUGGESTION_CATEGORIES)
    18→        .describe(
    19→          "optimization: improve existing approach. risk: potential problem to address. opportunity: beneficial possibility. conflict: contradictory elements detected. missing: gap in current plan. pivot: fundamental direction change worth considering.",
    20→        ),
    21→      rationale: z.string().min(1).describe("Why you are making this suggestion — the reasoning and evidence behind it"),
    22→      confidence: z.number().min(0).max(1).describe("How confident you are in this suggestion (0-1)"),
    23→      target_entity_id: z
    24→        .string()
    25→        .optional()
    26→        .describe("Optional target entity this suggestion is about (format: table:id, e.g. project:uuid or feature:uuid)"),
    27→      evidence_entity_ids: z
    28→        .array(z.string())
    29→        .optional()
    30→        .describe("Optional entity IDs that support the rationale (format: table:id). Can include observations, decisions, tasks, etc."),
    31→    }),
    32→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    33→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    34→
    35→      const targetTables = ["project", "feature", "task", "question", "decision"] as const;
    36→      const targetRecord = input.target_entity_id
    37→        ? parseRecordIdString(input.target_entity_id, [...targetTables])
    38→        : undefined;
    39→
    40→      if (targetRecord) {
    41→        const scoped = await isEntityInWorkspace(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, targetRecord);
    42→        if (!scoped) {
    43→          throw new Error("target entity is outside the current workspace scope");
    44→        }
    45→      }
    46→
    47→      const evidenceTables = ["workspace", "project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"] as const;
    48→      const evidenceRecords = input.evidence_entity_ids?.map((id) =>
    49→        parseRecordIdString(id, [...evidenceTables]),
    50→      );
    51→
    52→      const embedding = await createEmbeddingVector(deps.embeddingModel, input.text, deps.embeddingDimension);
    53→      if (!embedding) {
    54→        throw new Error("failed to create embedding for create_suggestion");
    55→      }
    56→
    57→      const suggestionRecord = await createSuggestion({
    58→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    59→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    60→        text: input.text,
    61→        category: input.category,
    62→        rationale: input.rationale,
    63→        suggestedBy: context.actor,
    64→        confidence: input.confidence,
    65→        now: new Date(),
    66→        sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    67→        ...(targetRecord ? { targetRecord } : {}),
    68→        ...(evidenceRecords ? { evidenceRecords } : {}),
    69→        embedding,
    70→      });
    71→
    72→      return {
    73→        suggestion_id: `suggestion:${suggestionRecord.id as string}`,
    74→        text: input.text,
    75→        category: input.category,
    76→        rationale: input.rationale,
    77→        confidence: input.confidence,
    78→        status: "pending",
    79→        ...(input.target_entity_id ? { target: input.target_entity_id } : {}),
    80→      };
    81→    },
    82→  });
    83→}
    84→
```

     1→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import { verifyApiKey } from "./api-key";
     3→import { jsonError } from "../http/response";
     4→
     5→type WorkspaceRow = {
     6→  id: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
     7→  name: string;
     8→  api_key_hash?: string;
     9→};
    10→
    11→export type McpAuthResult = {
    12→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    13→  workspaceName: string;
    14→};
    15→
    16→/**
    17→ * Authenticate an MCP request via Bearer token.
    18→ * Checks the Authorization header against workspace API key hashes.
    19→ * Returns workspace context on success, or an error Response.
    20→ */
    21→export async function authenticateMcpRequest(
    22→  request: Request,
    23→  workspaceId: string,
    24→  surreal: Surreal,
    25→): Promise<McpAuthResult | Response> {
    26→  const authHeader = request.headers.get("authorization");
    27→  if (!authHeader || !authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ")) {
    28→    return jsonError("missing or invalid Authorization header", 401);
    29→  }
    30→
    31→  const apiKey = authHeader.slice(7);
    32→  if (!apiKey) {
    33→    return jsonError("empty API key", 401);
    34→  }
    35→
    36→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", workspaceId);
    37→  const workspace = await surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    38→  if (!workspace) {
    39→    return jsonError("workspace not found", 404);
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  if (!workspace.api_key_hash) {
    43→    return jsonError("workspace has no API key configured — run brain init", 403);
    44→  }
    45→
    46→  const valid = await verifyApiKey(apiKey, workspace.api_key_hash);
    47→  if (!valid) {
    48→    return jsonError("invalid API key", 401);
    49→  }
    50→
    51→  return {
    52→    workspaceRecord,
    53→    workspaceName: workspace.name,
    54→  };
    55→}
    56→
```

     1→import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import type { ChatMessageResponse } from "../../shared/contracts";
     4→import { HttpError } from "../http/errors";
     5→import { elapsedMs, logDebug, logError, logInfo, logWarn } from "../http/observability";
     6→import { parseIncomingMessageRequest } from "../http/parsing";
     7→import { jsonError, jsonResponse } from "../http/response";
     8→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
     9→import type { ConversationRow, WorkspaceRow } from "../extraction/types";
    10→import { parseRecordIdString } from "../graph/queries";
    11→import { resolveWorkspaceRecord } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    12→import { deriveMessageTitle } from "../workspace/conversation-sidebar";
    13→import { processChatMessage } from "./chat-processor";
    14→
    15→export function createChatIngressHandlers(deps: ServerDependencies): {
    16→  handlePostChatMessage: (request: Request) => Promise<Response>;
    17→  handleChatStream: (messageId: string) => Response;
    18→} {
    19→  return {
    20→    handlePostChatMessage: (request: Request) => handlePostChatMessage(deps, request),
    21→    handleChatStream: (messageId: string) => deps.sse.handleStreamRequest(messageId),
    22→  };
    23→}
    24→
    25→async function handlePostChatMessage(deps: ServerDependencies, request: Request): Promise<Response> {
    26→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    27→  logInfo("chat.message.ingress.started", "Chat message ingress started");
    28→
    29→  let parsed: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof parseIncomingMessageRequest>>;
    30→  try {
    31→    parsed = await parseIncomingMessageRequest(request);
    32→  } catch (error) {
    33→    logError("chat.message.parse.failed", "Parsing incoming chat message failed", error);
    34→    const errorText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "invalid request body";
    35→    return jsonError(errorText, 400);
    36→  }
    37→
    38→  if (!parsed.ok) {
    39→    return jsonError(parsed.error, 400);
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const conversationId = parsed.data.conversationId ?? randomUUID();
    43→  const messageId = randomUUID();
    44→  const workspaceId = parsed.data.workspaceId;
    45→  const userText = parsed.data.text.trim();
    46→  const onboardingAction = parsed.data.onboardingAction;
    47→  const messageText = userText.length > 0 ? userText : `Uploaded document: ${parsed.data.attachment?.fileName ?? "attachment"}`;
    48→  const userMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", randomUUID());
    49→
    50→  logDebug("http.request.validated", "Chat message request validated", {
    51→    workspaceId,
    52→    conversationId,
    53→    hasAttachment: parsed.data.attachment !== undefined,
    54→  });
    55→
    56→  let workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    57→
    58→  try {
    59→    logInfo("chat.message.persist.started", "Persisting user chat message", {
    60→      workspaceId,
    61→      conversationId,
    62→      messageId,
    63→    });
    64→
    65→    workspaceRecord = await resolveWorkspaceRecord(deps.surreal, workspaceId);
    66→    const workspace = await deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(workspaceRecord);
    67→    if (!workspace) {
    68→      throw new HttpError(404, `workspace not found: ${workspaceId}`);
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    const discussEntityTables = ["project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"] as const;
    72→    const discussesRecord = parsed.data.discussEntityId
    73→      ? parseRecordIdString(parsed.data.discussEntityId, [...discussEntityTables])
    74→      : undefined;
    75→
    76→    const now = new Date();
    77→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", conversationId);
    78→    const existingConversation = await deps.surreal.select<ConversationRow>(conversationRecord);
    79→
    80→    const transaction = await deps.surreal.beginTransaction();
    81→    try {
    82→      if (existingConversation) {
    83→        if (!existingConversation.workspace) {
    84→          throw new HttpError(500, "conversation is missing workspace scope");
    85→        }
    86→
    87→        if (existingConversation.workspace.id !== workspaceRecord.id) {
    88→          throw new HttpError(400, "conversation scope does not match workspaceId");
    89→        }
    90→
    91→        await transaction.update(conversationRecord).merge({
    92→          updatedAt: now,
    93→        });
    94→      } else {
    95→        await transaction.create(conversationRecord).content({
    96→          createdAt: now,
    97→          updatedAt: now,
    98→          workspace: workspaceRecord,
    99→          title: deriveMessageTitle(messageText),
   100→          title_source: "message",
   101→          ...(workspace.onboarding_complete ? {} : { source: "onboarding" }),
   102→          ...(discussesRecord ? { discusses: discussesRecord } : {}),
   103→        });
   104→      }
   105→
   106→      await transaction.create(userMessageRecord).content({
   107→        conversation: conversationRecord,
   108→        role: "user",
   109→        text: messageText,
   110→        createdAt: now,
   111→        clientMessageId: parsed.data.clientMessageId,
   112→      });
   113→
   114→      if (!workspace.onboarding_complete) {
   115→        await transaction.update(workspaceRecord).merge({
   116→          onboarding_turn_count: workspace.onboarding_turn_count + 1,
   117→          updated_at: now,
   118→        });
   119→      }
   120→
   121→      await transaction.commit();
   122→    } catch (error) {
   123→      await transaction.cancel();
   124→      throw error;
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    logInfo("chat.message.persist.completed", "User chat message persisted", {
   128→      workspaceId,
   129→      conversationId,
   130→      messageId,
   131→      userMessageId: userMessageRecord.id as string,
   132→    });
   133→  } catch (error) {
   134→    if (error instanceof HttpError) {
   135→      logWarn("chat.message.persist.http_error", "Chat message persistence failed with client-facing error", {
   136→        workspaceId,
   137→        conversationId,
   138→        messageId,
   139→        statusCode: error.status,
   140→      });
   141→      return jsonError(error.message, error.status);
   142→    }
   143→
   144→    logError("chat.message.persist.failed", "Persisting user chat message failed", error, {
   145→      workspaceId,
   146→      conversationId,
   147→      messageId,
   148→    });
   149→    const errorText = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "failed to persist user message";
   150→    return jsonError(errorText, 500);
   151→  }
   152→
   153→  deps.sse.registerMessage(messageId);
   154→
   155→  logInfo("chat.message.process.started", "Async chat processing started", {
   156→    workspaceId,
   157→    conversationId,
   158→    messageId,
   159→  });
   160→
   161→  void processChatMessage({
   162→    deps,
   163→    conversationId,
   164→    messageId,
   165→    workspaceRecord,
   166→    userMessageRecord,
   167→    userText: messageText,
   168→    attachment: parsed.data.attachment,
   169→    ...(onboardingAction ? { onboardingAction } : {}),
   170→  });
   171→
   172→  const response: ChatMessageResponse = {
   173→    messageId,
   174→    userMessageId: userMessageRecord.id as string,
   175→    conversationId,
   176→    workspaceId,
   177→    streamUrl: `/api/chat/stream/${messageId}`,
   178→  };
   179→
   180→  logInfo("chat.message.ingress.completed", "Chat message ingress completed", {
   181→    workspaceId,
   182→    conversationId,
   183→    messageId,
   184→    durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   185→  });
   186→
   187→  return jsonResponse(response, 200);
   188→}
   189→
```

     1→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { ExtractedEntity, ExtractedRelationship, OnboardingAction } from "../../shared/contracts";
     3→import { loadAssistantConversationContext } from "../extraction/context-loaders";
     4→import { loadBranchChain, loadMessagesWithInheritance } from "./branch-chain";
     5→import { ingestAttachment } from "../extraction/document-ingestion";
     6→import { createEmbedding, persistEmbeddings } from "../extraction/embedding-writeback";
     7→import { appendExtractedTools } from "../extraction/persist-extraction";
     8→import type { ConversationRow, GraphEntityRecord, IncomingAttachment, WorkspaceRow } from "../extraction/types";
     9→import { elapsedMs, logError, logInfo, userFacingError } from "../http/observability";
    10→import { transitionOnboardingState } from "../onboarding/onboarding-state";
    11→import type { ServerDependencies } from "../runtime/types";
    12→import { runChatAgent } from "./handler";
    13→import { getWorkspaceOwnerRecord } from "../graph/queries";
    14→import { refreshConversationTouchedBy, maybeUpgradeConversationTitle } from "../workspace/conversation-sidebar";
    15→import { loadWorkspaceProjects } from "../workspace/workspace-scope";
    16→
    17→export async function processChatMessage(input: {
    18→  deps: ServerDependencies;
    19→  conversationId: string;
    20→  messageId: string;
    21→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    22→  userMessageRecord: RecordId<"message", string>;
    23→  userText: string;
    24→  attachment?: IncomingAttachment;
    25→  onboardingAction?: OnboardingAction;
    26→}): Promise<void> {
    27→  const startedAt = performance.now();
    28→  logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.started", "Chat message processing execution started", {
    29→    conversationId: input.conversationId,
    30→    messageId: input.messageId,
    31→    workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
    32→    hasAttachment: input.attachment !== undefined,
    33→  });
    34→
    35→  try {
    36→    const now = new Date();
    37→    const conversationRecord = new RecordId("conversation", input.conversationId);
    38→    const workspace = await input.deps.surreal.select<WorkspaceRow>(input.workspaceRecord);
    39→    if (!workspace) {
    40→      throw new Error("workspace not found");
    41→    }
    42→
    43→    const conversation = await input.deps.surreal.select<ConversationRow>(conversationRecord);
    44→    if (!conversation) {
    45→      throw new Error("conversation not found");
    46→    }
    47→
    48→    // Check if this conversation is a branch and load inherited context
    49→    const branchChain = await loadBranchChain(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId);
    50→    const isBranch = branchChain.length > 0;
    51→
    52→    let inheritedEntityIds: RecordId[] | undefined;
    53→    let assistantContextRows: Array<{ id: RecordId<"message", string>; role: "user" | "assistant"; text: string; createdAt: Date | string }>;
    54→
    55→    if (isBranch) {
    56→      // For branches, load full message history including inherited messages
    57→      const allMessages = await loadMessagesWithInheritance(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId, 30);
    58→      assistantContextRows = allMessages
    59→        .map((m) => ({
    60→          id: new RecordId("message", m.id),
    61→          role: m.role,
    62→          text: m.text,
    63→          createdAt: m.createdAt,
    64→        }))
    65→        .slice(-10);
    66→
    67→      // Derive inherited entity IDs from extraction_relations on inherited messages
    68→      const inheritedMsgIds = allMessages
    69→        .filter((m) => m.inherited)
    70→        .map((m) => new RecordId("message", m.id));
    71→
    72→      if (inheritedMsgIds.length > 0) {
    73→        const [entityRows] = await input.deps.surreal
    74→          .query<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>(
    75→            "SELECT DISTINCT out FROM extraction_relation WHERE `in` IN $msgIds LIMIT 30;",
    76→            { msgIds: inheritedMsgIds },
    77→          )
    78→          .collect<[Array<{ out: RecordId }>]>();
    79→        inheritedEntityIds = entityRows.map((r) => r.out);
    80→      }
    81→    } else {
    82→      assistantContextRows = await loadAssistantConversationContext(input.deps.surreal, input.conversationId);
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    // Embed user message early: used for context enrichment and persisted to the message record
    86→    const userMessageEmbedding = await createEmbedding(input.deps.embeddingModel, input.deps.config.embeddingDimension, input.userText);
    87→    if (userMessageEmbedding) {
    88→      void input.deps.surreal
    89→        .query("UPDATE $record MERGE { embedding: $embedding };", {
    90→          record: input.userMessageRecord,
    91→          embedding: userMessageEmbedding,
    92→        })
    93→        .catch(() => undefined);
    94→    }
    95→
    96→    const workspaceProjects = await loadWorkspaceProjects(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord);
    97→    const workspaceProjectNames = workspaceProjects.map((project) => project.name);
    98→    const persistedEntities: ExtractedEntity[] = [];
    99→    const persistedRelationships: ExtractedRelationship[] = [];
   100→    const embeddingTargets: Array<{ record: GraphEntityRecord; text: string }> = [];
   101→    const extractedTools: string[] = [];
   102→    const unresolvedAssigneeNames = new Set<string>();
   103→
   104→    // Attachment extraction stays automatic — document ingestion is intentional
   105→    if (input.attachment) {
   106→      const ingestion = await ingestAttachment({
   107→        surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   108→        extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
   109→        embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   110→        embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   111→        extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   112→        extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   113→        workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   114→        conversationRecord,
   115→        userMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   116→        attachment: input.attachment,
   117→        workspaceName: workspace.name,
   118→        projectNames: workspaceProjectNames,
   119→        now,
   120→        onChunkResult: (chunkResult) => {
   121→          if (chunkResult.entities.length > 0 || chunkResult.relationships.length > 0) {
   122→            input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   123→              type: "extraction",
   124→              messageId: input.messageId,
   125→              entities: chunkResult.entities,
   126→              relationships: chunkResult.relationships,
   127→            });
   128→          }
   129→        },
   130→      });
   131→
   132→      persistedEntities.push(...ingestion.entities);
   133→      persistedRelationships.push(...ingestion.relationships);
   134→      embeddingTargets.push(...ingestion.embeddingTargets);
   135→      extractedTools.push(...ingestion.tools);
   136→      for (const unresolvedName of ingestion.unresolvedAssigneeNames) {
   137→        unresolvedAssigneeNames.add(unresolvedName);
   138→      }
   139→    }
   140→
   141→    if (extractedTools.length > 0) {
   142→      await appendExtractedTools(input.deps.surreal, input.workspaceRecord, extractedTools, now);
   143→    }
   144→
   145→    const onboardingBefore = workspace.onboarding_complete
   146→      ? "complete"
   147→      : workspace.onboarding_summary_pending
   148→        ? "summary_pending"
   149→        : "active";
   150→
   151→    const onboardingAfter = await transitionOnboardingState({
   152→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   153→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   154→      workspace,
   155→      onboardingAction: input.onboardingAction,
   156→      now,
   157→    });
   158→
   159→    // Always run chat agent — it handles both onboarding and post-onboarding
   160→    const workspaceOwnerRecord = await getWorkspaceOwnerRecord({
   161→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   162→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   163→    });
   164→
   165→    const graphAwareResponse = await runChatAgent({
   166→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   167→      model: input.deps.chatAgentModel,
   168→      pmAgentModel: input.deps.pmAgentModel,
   169→      analyticsAgentModel: input.deps.analyticsAgentModel,
   170→      analyticsSurreal: input.deps.analyticsSurreal,
   171→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   172→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   173→      extractionModelId: input.deps.config.extractionModelId,
   174→      extractionModel: input.deps.extractionModel,
   175→      extractionStoreThreshold: input.deps.config.extractionStoreThreshold,
   176→      conversationRecord,
   177→      workspaceRecord: input.workspaceRecord,
   178→      currentMessageRecord: input.userMessageRecord,
   179→      latestUserText: input.userText,
   180→      isOnboarding: onboardingAfter !== "complete",
   181→      onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   182→      ...(workspaceOwnerRecord ? { workspaceOwnerRecord } : {}),
   183→      ...(userMessageEmbedding ? { userMessageEmbedding } : {}),
   184→      ...(inheritedEntityIds && inheritedEntityIds.length > 0 ? { inheritedEntityIds } : {}),
   185→      ...(conversation.discusses ? { discussesRecord: conversation.discusses } : {}),
   186→      messages: assistantContextRows.map((row) => ({
   187→        role: row.role,
   188→        text: row.text,
   189→      })),
   190→      onToken: async (token) => {
   191→        input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   192→          type: "token",
   193→          messageId: input.messageId,
   194→          token,
   195→        });
   196→      },
   197→      onReasoning: async (token) => {
   198→        input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   199→          type: "reasoning",
   200→          messageId: input.messageId,
   201→          token,
   202→        });
   203→      },
   204→    });
   205→
   206→    let assistantText = graphAwareResponse.text.trim();
   207→    persistedEntities.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedEntities);
   208→    persistedRelationships.push(...graphAwareResponse.collectedRelationships);
   209→
   210→    const unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions = [...unresolvedAssigneeNames].map(
   211→      (name) => `You mentioned ${name} - want to add them to workspace people?`,
   212→    );
   213→    const assistantSuggestions = sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(unresolvedAssigneeSuggestions, 3);
   214→
   215→    // Post-response hooks (entities now created during chat agent execution)
   216→    await refreshConversationTouchedBy(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   217→    await maybeUpgradeConversationTitle(input.deps.surreal, conversationRecord);
   218→
   219→    if (assistantText.trim().length === 0) {
   220→      assistantText = "I could not generate a response for that request.";
   221→    }
   222→
   223→    const assistantMessageRecord = new RecordId("message", input.messageId);
   224→    await input.deps.surreal.create(assistantMessageRecord).content({
   225→      conversation: conversationRecord,
   226→      role: "assistant",
   227→      text: assistantText,
   228→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   229→      createdAt: now,
   230→    });
   231→
   232→    await input.deps.surreal.update(conversationRecord).merge({
   233→      updatedAt: now,
   234→    });
   235→
   236→    void persistEmbeddings({
   237→      surreal: input.deps.surreal,
   238→      embeddingModel: input.deps.embeddingModel,
   239→      embeddingDimension: input.deps.config.embeddingDimension,
   240→      assistantMessageRecord,
   241→      assistantText,
   242→      entities: embeddingTargets,
   243→    }).catch(() => undefined);
   244→
   245→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   246→      type: "extraction",
   247→      messageId: input.messageId,
   248→      entities: persistedEntities,
   249→      relationships: persistedRelationships,
   250→    });
   251→
   252→    if (onboardingBefore !== onboardingAfter) {
   253→      input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   254→        type: "onboarding_state",
   255→        messageId: input.messageId,
   256→        onboardingState: onboardingAfter,
   257→      });
   258→    }
   259→
   260→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   261→      type: "assistant_message",
   262→      messageId: input.messageId,
   263→      text: assistantText,
   264→      ...(assistantSuggestions.length > 0 ? { suggestions: assistantSuggestions } : {}),
   265→    });
   266→
   267→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   268→      type: "done",
   269→      messageId: input.messageId,
   270→    });
   271→
   272→    logInfo("chat.message.process.execution.completed", "Chat message processing execution completed", {
   273→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   274→      messageId: input.messageId,
   275→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   276→      entityCount: persistedEntities.length,
   277→      relationshipCount: persistedRelationships.length,
   278→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   279→    });
   280→  } catch (error) {
   281→    logError("chat.message.process.execution.failed", "Chat message processing execution failed", error, {
   282→      conversationId: input.conversationId,
   283→      messageId: input.messageId,
   284→      workspaceId: input.workspaceRecord.id as string,
   285→      durationMs: elapsedMs(startedAt),
   286→    });
   287→    const errorText = userFacingError(error, "chat processing failed");
   288→    input.deps.sse.emitEvent(input.messageId, {
   289→      type: "error",
   290→      messageId: input.messageId,
   291→      error: errorText,
   292→    });
   293→  }
   294→}
   295→
   296→function sanitizeAssistantSuggestions(suggestions: string[], limit: number): string[] {
   297→  return [...new Set(suggestions.map((value) => value.trim()))]
   298→    .filter((value) => value.length > 0 && value.length <= 140)
   299→    .slice(0, limit);
   300→}
   301→

{
  "file_path": "/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain/riga/app/src/server/iam/identity.ts",
  "content": "import { RecordId, type Surreal } from \"surrealdb\";\n\nexport async function resolveIdentity(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  provider: string;\n  providerId: string;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n}): Promise<RecordId<\"person\", string> | undefined> {\n  const [rows] = await input.surreal.query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<\"person\", string> }>]>(\n    `SELECT id FROM person\n     WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM member_of WHERE out = $workspace)\n       AND identities[WHERE provider = $provider AND id = $providerId]\n     LIMIT 1;`,\n    {\n      workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n      provider: input.provider,\n      providerId: input.providerId,\n    },\n  );\n\n  return rows.length > 0 ? rows[0].id : undefined;\n}\n\nexport async function resolveByEmail(input: {\n  surreal: Surreal;\n  email: string;\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>;\n}): Promise<RecordId<\"person\", string> | undefined> {\n  const normalizedEmail = input.email.trim().toLowerCase();\n  if (normalizedEmail.length === 0) return undefined;\n\n  const [rows] = await input.surreal.query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<\"person\", string> }>]>(\n    `SELECT id FROM person\n     WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE \\`in\\` FROM member_of WHERE out = $workspace)\n       AND string::lowercase(contact_email) = $email\n     LIMIT 1;`,\n    {\n      workspace: input.workspaceRecord,\n      email: normalizedEmail,\n    },\n  );\n\n  return rows.length > 0 ? rows[0].id : undefined;\n}\n"
}

     1→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     2→import type { PersistableExtractableEntityKind } from "./types";
     3→
     4→export type PersonAttributionPatch =
     5→  | { kind: "feature"; field: "owner"; value: string }
     6→  | { kind: "feature"; field: "owner_name"; value: string }
     7→  | { kind: "task"; field: "owner"; value: string }
     8→  | { kind: "task"; field: "owner_name"; value: string }
     9→  | { kind: "decision"; field: "decided_by"; value: string }
    10→  | { kind: "decision"; field: "decided_by_name"; value: string }
    11→  | { kind: "question"; field: "assigned_to"; value: string }
    12→  | { kind: "question"; field: "assigned_to_name"; value: string };
    13→
    14→export async function findWorkspacePersonByName(input: {
    15→  surreal: Surreal;
    16→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>;
    17→  personName: string;
    18→}): Promise<RecordId<"person", string> | undefined> {
    19→  const normalizedName = input.personName.trim();
    20→  if (normalizedName.length === 0) {
    21→    return undefined;
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  const [rows] = await input.surreal
    25→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string> }>]>(
    26→      [
    27→        "SELECT id",
    28→        "FROM person",
    29→        "WHERE id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM member_of WHERE out = $workspace)",
    30→        "AND string::lowercase(name) = string::lowercase($name)",
    31→        "LIMIT 1;",
    32→      ].join(" "),
    33→      {
    34→        workspace: input.workspaceRecord,
    35→        name: normalizedName,
    36→      },
    37→    )
    38→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"person", string> }>]>() ;
    39→
    40→  return rows[0]?.id;
    41→}
    42→
    43→export function resolvePersonAttributionPatch(input: {
    44→  targetKind: PersistableExtractableEntityKind;
    45→  assigneeName: string;
    46→  personRecordId?: string;
    47→}): PersonAttributionPatch {
    48→  if (input.targetKind === "feature") {
    49→    if (input.personRecordId) {
    50→      return { kind: "feature", field: "owner", value: input.personRecordId };
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    return { kind: "feature", field: "owner_name", value: input.assigneeName };
    54→  }
    55→
    56→  if (input.targetKind === "task") {
    57→    if (input.personRecordId) {
    58→      return { kind: "task", field: "owner", value: input.personRecordId };
    59→    }
    60→
    61→    return { kind: "task", field: "owner_name", value: input.assigneeName };
    62→  }
    63→
    64→  if (input.targetKind === "decision") {
    65→    if (input.personRecordId) {
    66→      return { kind: "decision", field: "decided_by", value: input.personRecordId };
    67→    }
    68→
    69→    return { kind: "decision", field: "decided_by_name", value: input.assigneeName };
    70→  }
    71→
    72→  if (input.personRecordId) {
    73→    return { kind: "question", field: "assigned_to", value: input.personRecordId };
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  return { kind: "question", field: "assigned_to_name", value: input.assigneeName };
    77→}
    78→

    49→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.query(`DEFINE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`), 10_000, "define test namespace");
    50→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.use({ namespace }), 10_000, "switch to test namespace");
    51→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.query(`DEFINE DATABASE ${database};`), 10_000, "define test database");
    52→    await withTimeout(
    53→      () => surreal.use({ namespace, database }),
    54→      10_000,

    59→    await withTimeout(() => surreal.query(schemaSql), 20_000, "apply schema");

```

> AGENT

The issue is `DEFINE TABLE OVERWRITE` inside a transaction — the base schema already defines the table, and `OVERWRITE` inside a transaction can fail. The migration should use `DEFINE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` since the base schema already has it, or skip the table definition entirely and just seed data. Per AGENTS.md, the migration pattern is `DEFINE ... OVERWRITE` but the base schema already defines the table. The migration should just seed.

> TOOL

new Surreal();\n  await surreal.connect(surrealUrl);\n  await surreal.signin({ username: surrealUsername, password: surrealPassword });\n  await surreal.query(`DEFINE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`);\n  await surreal.use({ namespace });\n  await surreal.query(`DEFINE DATABASE ${database};`);\n  await surreal.use({ namespace, database });\n\n  // Apply base schema\n  const schemaSql = readFileSync(join(process.cwd(), \"schema\", \"surreal-schema.surql\"), \"utf8\");\n  await surreal.query(schemaSql);\n\n  // Apply migrations, ignoring failures from already-applied schema\n  const migrationsDir = join(process.cwd(), \"schema\", \"migrations\");\n  const migrationFiles = readdirSync(migrationsDir)\n    .filter((f) => f.endsWith(\".surql\"))\n    .sort();\n  for (const file of migrationFiles) {\n    const migrationSql = readFileSync(join(migrationsDir, file), \"utf8\");\n    try {\n      await surreal.query(migrationSql);\n    } catch {\n      // Migrations may fail if schema already applied — expected\n    }\n  }\n}, 30_000);\n\nafterAll(async () => {\n  if (!process.env.SMOKE_KEEP_DB) {\n    try {\n      await surreal.query(`REMOVE DATABASE ${database};`);\n    } catch {}\n    try {\n      await surreal.query(`REMOVE NAMESPACE ${namespace};`);\n    } catch {}\n  }\n  surreal.close();\n});\n\ndescribe(\"checkAuthority\", () => {\n  it(\"returns auto for code_agent create_task\", async () => {\n    const result = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"code_agent\",\n      action: \"create_task\",\n    });\n    expect(result).toBe(\"auto\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns blocked for code_agent confirm_decision\", async () => {\n    const result = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"code_agent\",\n      action: \"confirm_decision\",\n    });\n    expect(result).toBe(\"blocked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns provisional for code_agent create_decision\", async () => {\n    const result = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"code_agent\",\n      action: \"create_decision\",\n    });\n    expect(result).toBe(\"provisional\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns blocked for observer create_task\", async () => {\n    const result = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"observer\",\n      action: \"create_task\",\n    });\n    expect(result).toBe(\"blocked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns auto for observer create_observation\", async () => {\n    const result = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"observer\",\n      action: \"create_observation\",\n    });\n    expect(result).toBe(\"auto\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns provisional for design_partner create_task\", async () => {\n    const result = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"design_partner\",\n      action: \"create_task\",\n    });\n    expect(result).toBe(\"provisional\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"returns blocked for unknown action\", async () => {\n    const result = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"code_agent\",\n      // @ts-expect-error — testing unknown action fallback\n      action: \"nonexistent_action\",\n    });\n    expect(result).toBe(\"blocked\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"workspace-specific override takes precedence\", async () => {\n    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", \"test-ws-override\");\n    await surreal.create(workspaceRecord).content({\n      name: \"test-override\",\n      created_at: new Date(),\n      updated_at: new Date(),\n      onboarding_complete: true,\n      onboarding_turn_count: 0,\n    });\n\n    // Global default is auto\n    const globalResult = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"code_agent\",\n      action: \"create_task\",\n    });\n    expect(globalResult).toBe(\"auto\");\n\n    // Create workspace-specific override to blocked\n    await surreal.query(\n      `CREATE authority_scope CONTENT {\n        agent_type: \"code_agent\",\n        action: \"create_task\",\n        permission: \"blocked\",\n        workspace: $workspace,\n        created_at: time::now()\n      };`,\n      { workspace: workspaceRecord },\n    );\n\n    // Workspace-specific should win\n    const wsResult = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"code_agent\",\n      action: \"create_task\",\n      workspaceRecord,\n    });\n    expect(wsResult).toBe(\"blocked\");\n\n    // Without workspace, still global default\n    const stillGlobal = await checkAuthority({\n      surreal,\n      agentType: \"code_agent\",\n      action: \"create_task\",\n    });\n    expect(stillGlobal).toBe(\"auto\");\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe(\"identity resolution\", () => {\n  it(\"resolveByEmail finds person by case-insensitive email\", async () => {\n    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", \"test-ws-identity\");\n    const personRecord = new RecordId(\"person\", \"test-person-email\");\n\n    await surreal.create(workspaceRecord).content({\n      name: \"test-identity\",\n      created_at: new Date(),\n      updated_at: new Date(),\n      onboarding_complete: true,\n      onboarding_turn_count: 0,\n    });\n\n    await surreal.create(personRecord).content({\n      name: \"Test User\",\n      contact_email: \"Test@Example.com\",\n      email_verified: false,\n      created_at: new Date(),\n      updated_at: new Date(),\n    });\n\n    await surreal\n      .relate(personRecord, new RecordId(\"member_of\", \"test-membership\"), workspaceRecord, {\n        role: \"member\",\n        added_at: new Date(),\n      })\n      .output(\"after\");\n\n    const result = await resolveByEmail({\n      surreal,\n      email: \"test@example.com\",\n      workspaceRecord,\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeDefined();\n    expect(result!.id).toBe(\"test-person-email\");\n  });\n\n  it(\"resolveByEmail returns undefined for non-member\", async () => {\n    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", \"test-ws-nonmember\");\n    const personRecord = new RecordId(\"person\", \"test-person-nonmember\");\n\n    await surreal.create(workspaceRecord).content({\n      name: \"test-nonmember\",\n      created_at: new Date(),\n      updated_at: new Date(),\n      onboarding_complete: true,\n      onboarding_turn_count: 0,\n    });\n\n    await surreal.create(personRecord).content({\n      name: \"Outsider\",\n      contact_email: \"outsider@example.com\",\n      email_verified: false,\n      created_at: new Date(),\n      updated_at: new Date(),\n    });\n\n    const result = await resolveByEmail({\n      surreal,\n      email: \"outsider@example.com\",\n      workspaceRecord,\n    });\n\n    expect(result).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n\n  it(\"resolveWorkspacePerson chains name then email fallback\", async () => {\n    const workspaceRecord = new RecordId(\"workspace\", \"test-ws-composite\");\n    const personRecord = new RecordId(\"person\", \"test-person-composite\");\n\n    await surreal.create(workspaceRecord).content({\n      name: \"test-composite\",\n      created_at: new Date(),\n      updated_at: new Date(),\n      onboarding_complete: true,\n      onboarding_turn_count: 0,\n    });\n\n    await surreal.create(personRecord).content({\n      name: \"Alice\",\n      contact_email: \"alice@example.com\",\n      email_verified: false,\n      created_at: new Date(),\n      updated_at: new Date(),\n    });\n\n    await surreal\n      .relate(personRecord, new RecordId(\"member_of\", \"test-composite-member\"), workspaceRecord, {\n        role: \"member\",\n        added_at: new Date(),\n      })\n      .output(\"after\");\n\n    // Name match\n    const byName = await resolveWorkspacePerson({\n      surreal,\n      workspaceRecord,\n      personName: \"Alice\",\n    });\n    expect(byName).toBeDefined();\n    expect(byName!.id).toBe(\"test-person-composite\");\n\n    // Email fallback\n    const byEmail = await resolveWorkspacePerson({\n      surreal,\n      workspaceRecord,\n      personName: \"alice@example.com\",\n    });\n    expect(byEmail).toBeDefined();\n    expect(byEmail!.id).toBe(\"test-person-composite\");\n\n    // No match\n    const noMatch = await resolveWorkspacePerson({\n      surreal,\n      workspaceRecord,\n      personName: \"nobody\",\n    });\n    expect(noMatch).toBeUndefined();\n  });\n});\n"
}

     1→# IAM Phase 1: MVP — Ship Now
     2→
     3→**Goal:** Basic identity that doesn't block anything else. API key for dogfooding, OAuth-ready architecture.
     4→
     5→**Status:** In progress — better-auth + SurrealDB adapter shipped (PR #98).
     6→
     7→## What's Done
     8→
     9→- [x] Person table evolved with auth fields (`contact_email`, `email_verified`, `image`, `updated_at`)
    10→- [x] better-auth integrated with custom SurrealDB v2 adapter
    11→- [x] GitHub OAuth provider configured (sign-in redirect, account linking)
    12→- [x] Session, account, verification tables created
    13→- [x] Account linking works (existing person + new OAuth provider)
    14→- [x] 17 integration tests passing
    15→
    16→## What's Left
    17→
    18→### Identity Resolution (exact match only)
    19→
    20→Resolve external identifiers to Person nodes via two strategies:
    21→
    22→1. **Exact provider match** — `account.provider_id == id AND account.provider_id == provider`
    23→2. **Email match (cross-provider)** — `person.contact_email == email`
    24→
    25→No fuzzy matching in Phase 1. Unresolved names stored as string attributes on entities.
    26→
    27→```sql
    28→-- Resolve an external identity to a Person
    29→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::resolve_identity($provider: string, $provider_id: string) {
    30→  LET $person = SELECT * FROM person
    31→    WHERE identities[WHERE provider = $provider AND provider_id = $provider_id];
    32→
    33→  IF array::len($person) > 0 {
    34→    RETURN $person[0];
    35→  };
    36→
    37→  RETURN NONE;
    38→};
    39→
    40→-- Resolve by email (cross-provider)
    41→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::resolve_by_email($email: string) {
    42→  LET $by_identity = SELECT * FROM person
    43→    WHERE identities[WHERE email = $email];
    44→
    45→  IF array::len($by_identity) > 0 {
    46→    RETURN $by_identity[0];
    47→  };
    48→
    49→  LET $by_contact = SELECT * FROM person
    50→    WHERE contact_info.email = $email;
    51→
    52→  IF array::len($by_contact) > 0 {
    53→    RETURN $by_contact[0];
    54→  };
    55→
    56→  RETURN NONE;
    57→};
    58→```
    59→
    60→### MCP Auth (API key — dogfooding only)
    61→
    62→Simple API key that maps to workspace + fixed scopes matching the default authority table. The MCP server's authorization layer validates scopes the same way whether they come from an API key or an OAuth token. Swapping to OAuth later means changing token validation, not rewriting authorization.
    63→
    64→### Authority Scopes (hardcoded defaults)
    65→
    66→Hardcoded defaults per agent type. Not configurable yet — just enforced.
    67→
    68→| Action | Code Agent | Architect | Management | Design Partner | Observer |
    69→|--------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|----------|
    70→| create_decision | provisional | provisional | provisional | provisional | blocked |
    71→| confirm_decision | blocked | blocked* | blocked | blocked | blocked |
    72→| create_task | auto | auto | auto | provisional | blocked |
    73→| complete_task | auto | blocked | auto | blocked | blocked |
    74→| create_subtask | auto | blocked | blocked | blocked | blocked |
    75→| create_observation | auto | auto | auto | auto | auto |
    76→| ask_question | auto | auto | auto | auto | blocked |
    77→| modify_feature | blocked | propose | propose | propose | blocked |
    78→| modify_project | blocked | propose | propose | blocked | blocked |
    79→
    80→Permission levels: **auto** (done, shown in feed), **provisional** (done as draft, needs review), **propose** (proposed, needs approval), **blocked** (cannot do).
    81→
    82→```sql
    83→-- Check if an agent action is allowed
    84→DEFINE FUNCTION fn::check_authority($agent_type: string, $workspace: record<workspace>, $action: string) {
    85→  LET $scope = SELECT * FROM authority_scope
    86→    WHERE agent_type = $agent_type AND workspace = $workspace;
    87→
    88→  IF array::len($scope) == 0 {
    89→    RETURN "provisional";
    90→  };
    91→
    92→  LET $permission = $scope[0].actions[$action];
    93→
    94→  IF $permission == NONE {
    95→    RETURN "blocked";
    96→  };
    97→
    98→  RETURN $permission;
    99→};
   100→```
   101→
   102→### Schema additions
   103→
   104→```sql
   105→-- Authority scopes
   106→DEFINE TABLE authority_scope SCHEMAFULL;
   107→DEFINE FIELD agent_type ON authority_scope TYPE string;
   108→DEFINE FIELD workspace ON authority_scope TYPE record<workspace>;
   109→DEFINE FIELD actions ON authority_scope TYPE object;
   110→DEFINE FIELD constraints ON authority_scope TYPE option<object>;
   111→
   112→-- Workspace permissions (owner-only for now)
   113→DEFINE TABLE workspace_permission SCHEMAFULL;
   114→DEFINE FIELD person ON workspace_permission TYPE record<person>;
   115→DEFINE FIELD workspace ON workspace_permission TYPE record<workspace>;
   116→DEFINE FIELD role ON workspace_permission TYPE string
   117→  ASSERT $value IN ["owner", "admin", "member", "viewer"];
   118→DEFINE FIELD project_access ON workspace_permission TYPE option<array<object>>;
   119→```
   120→
   121→### Platform-Managed Agent Auth
   122→
   123→`human_present` flag on web chat sessions. Chat agent can confirm decisions (human is live approval mechanism). Coding agent cannot.
   124→
   125→```
   126→AgentIdentity {
   127→  agent_id: string             // "architect:ws:xyz"
   128→  agent_type: string           // "architect" | "management" | "design-partner" | "observer"
   129→  workspace: string
   130→  authenticated_via: "platform"
   131→  human_present: boolean       // true for web chat, false for background
   132→}
   133→```
   134→
   135→### Web Chat Authentication
   136→
   137→Web chat agents authenticate via the user's browser session (session cookie from better-auth). Human is always present → `human_present: true`. The chat agent inherits the user's session permissions.
   138→
   139→### Extraction Pipeline — Name Resolution
   140→
   141→Extraction resolves names against existing Person nodes. Unresolved names stored as string attributes (`decided_by_name: "Sarah"`), suggestion surfaced in feed.
   142→
   143→**Critical rule:** Person nodes are never created by extraction. Only through explicit actions: workspace creation, OAuth connection, or manual invite.
   144→
   145→### GitHub OAuth Connection Flow
   146→
   147→```
   148→1. Human clicks "Connect GitHub" in workspace settings
   149→2. OAuth redirect → GitHub authorizes → callback with user ID + email + username
   150→3. System checks:
   151→   a. GitHub email matches existing Person? → auto-link (better-auth handles this)
   152→   b. No match? → link to currently logged-in Person
   153→4. Account record created with provider_id: "github", account_id: GitHub user ID
   154→5. OAuth tokens stored for API access
   155→```
   156→
   157→## What This Unblocks
   158→
   159→- Git commit attribution (post-commit hook → resolve author email → link to Person)
   160→- MCP tool authorization (coding agent can't confirm decisions)
   161→- Cross-source queries ("show me Marcus's commits and decisions this week")
   162→
```

  "new_string": "# IAM Phase 1: MVP — Ship Now\n\n**Goal:** Basic identity that doesn't block anything else. API key for dogfooding, OAuth-ready architecture.\n\n**Status:** Done.\n\n## What's Done\n\n- [x] Person table evolved with auth fields (`contact_email`, `email_verified`, `image`, `updated_at`)\n- [x] better-auth integrated with custom SurrealDB v2 adapter\n- [x] GitHub OAuth provider configured (sign-in redirect, account linking)\n- [x] Session, account, verification tables created\n- [x] Account linking works (existing person + new OAuth provider)\n- [x] 17 integration tests passing\n- [x] Identity resolution — exact provider match + case-insensitive email match (`app/src/server/iam/identity.ts`)\n- [x] Authority scopes — `authority_scope` table with 5 agent types × 9 actions, workspace-specific overrides (`schema/migrations/0011_authority_scope.surql`)\n- [x] Authority enforcement in chat tools — `requireAuthorizedContext` wrapper on all 10 write tools (`app/src/server/iam/authority.ts`)\n- [x] Authority enforcement in MCP routes — `checkAuthority` + `checkAuthorityOrError` on 7 write handlers, `X-Agent-Type` header support\n- [x] Web chat session auth — `humanPresent: true` bypasses authority (human IS the authority), session-resolved `personRecord`\n- [x] PM agent actor propagation — `pm_agent` maps to `management` agent type\n- [x] Extraction pipeline name resolution — composite resolver chains name match → email fallback (`resolveWorkspacePerson`)\n- [x] 11 smoke tests passing (`tests/smoke/authority.test.ts`)\n\n## Implementation Details\n\n### Identity Resolution (exact match only)\n\nImplemented in `app/src/server/iam/identity.ts` as app-layer queries (not SurrealDB stored functions, due to known SDK parameter binding limitations):\n\n1. **Exact provider match** — `resolveIdentity()`: queries `person.identities` array for matching provider + id, scoped to workspace via `member_of` edge\n2. **Email match (cross-provider)** — `resolveByEmail()`: case-insensitive `contact_email` match, scoped to workspace via `member_of` edge\n3. **Composite resolver** — `resolveWorkspacePerson()` in `app/src/server/extraction/person.ts`: chains exact name match → email fallback (if input contains `@`)\n\nNo fuzzy matching in Phase 1. Unresolved names stored as string attributes on entities.\n\n### MCP Auth (API key — dogfooding only)\n\nAPI key maps to workspace + agent type. `X-Agent-Type` header (default `\"code_agent\"`) controls authority scoping. Authorization enforced via `checkAuthority` before each write handler. Swapping to OAuth later means changing token validation, not rewriting authorization.\n\n### Authority Scopes (seeded defaults)\n\nSeeded in `schema/migrations/0011_authority_scope.surql` as 45 rows (5 agent types × 9 actions). Workspace-specific overrides supported via nullable `workspace` field (enables Phase 2 config UI without schema changes).\n\n| Action | Code Agent | Architect | Management | Design Partner | Observer |\n|--------|-----------|-----------|------------|----------------|----------|\n| create_decision | provisional | provisional | provisional | provisional | blocked |\n| confirm_decision | blocked | blocked | blocked | blocked | blocked |\n| create_task | auto | auto | auto | provisional | blocked |\n| complete_task | auto | blocked | auto | blocked | blocked |\n| create_observation | auto | auto | auto | auto | auto |\n| acknowledge_observation | auto | auto | auto | auto | blocked |\n| resolve_observation | blocked | auto | auto | blocked | blocked |\n| create_question | auto | auto | auto | auto | auto |\n| create_suggestion | auto | auto | auto | auto | blocked |\n\nPermission levels: **auto** (done, shown in feed), **provisional** (done as draft, needs review), **propose** (proposed, needs approval), **blocked** (cannot do).\n\n### Authority Enforcement\n\n- **Chat tools**: `requireAuthorizedContext(options, action, deps)` wraps `requireToolContext`, bypasses for `humanPresent === true`, maps actor to agent type, throws `AuthorityError` for blocked/propose\n- **MCP routes**: `checkAuthority()` + `checkAuthorityOrError()` returns 403 for blocked/propose\n- **Fail-safe**: no matching authority_scope row → `\"blocked\"`\n\n### Web Chat Authentication\n\nWeb chat requests set `humanPresent: true` on tool context — authority checks return `\"auto\"` (human at screen IS the authority). Session cookie resolved via `better-auth` to `personRecord` for attribution.\n\n### Platform-Managed Agent Auth\n\n`AgentType` union: `\"code_agent\" | \"architect\" | \"management\" | \"design_partner\" | \"observer\"`. Actor-to-agent mapping: `pm_agent → management`, `analytics_agent → observer`, default `code_agent`.\n\n### GitHub OAuth Connection Flow\n\n```\n1. Human clicks \"Connect GitHub\" in workspace settings\n2. OAuth redirect → GitHub authorizes → callback with user ID + email + username\n3. System checks:\n   a. GitHub email matches existing Person? → auto-link (better-auth handles this)\n   b. No match? → link to currently logged-in Person\n4. Account record created with provider_id: \"github\", account_id: GitHub user ID\n5. OAuth tokens stored for API access\n```\n\n## What This Unblocks\n\n- Git commit attribution (post-commit hook → resolve author email → link to Person)\n- MCP tool authorization (coding agent can't confirm decisions)\n- Cross-source queries (\"show me Marcus's commits and decisions this week\")\n- Phase 2: authority config UI (workspace-specific overrides already supported in schema)\n- Phase 2: OAuth 2.1 for MCP (swap token validation, authorization logic unchanged)"
}
